- **Service Discovery Research — Plan, Fieldwork & Synthesis**: Plans and synthesises discovery research for a service. Research is tied to the decision it informs and to falsifiable hypotheses. - **Service Ecosystem Mapping — Actors, Value Exchanges & Dependencies**: Maps the actors needed to deliver one service: customers, staff, partners, regulators, systems, and AI agents on either side. It surfaces misaligned incentives, single points of failure and the actors a redesign must win over.
